- **long-read-sv-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or reproducing SV caller performance on long-read (PacBio HiFi/CLR, ONT) datasets where a truth or high-confidence callset exists and recall/precision/F1/GT_F1 comparisons are the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-benchmarking))
- **long-read-sv-callset-qc**: Use when a VolcanoSV VCF from simulated long-read data (VISOR/PBSIM hg19) must be QC'd for INS-vs-DUP mislabeling, or when explaining/benchmarking elevated DUP F1 and GT_F1 against SVIM-asm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-callset-qc))
- **long-read-sv-callset-quality-verification**: Use when a Truvari-based SV caller ranking (e.g. sawfish vs Sniffles2 vs pbsv on HG002 32-fold HiFi WGS) needs confirmation with an independent benchmarking engine before trusting the conclusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-callset-quality-verification))
- **long-read-sv-consensus-evaluation**: Use when a collapsed consensus SV VCF (e.g. from ConsensuSV-ONT's vcfs output folder) must be labelled TP/FP against an HGSVC benchmark VCF to train or evaluate the CNN variant filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-consensus-evaluation))
- **long-read-sv-joint-genotyping**: Use when multiple mapped HiFi (CCS) sample BAMs are available and the goal is jointly genotyped SV calls whose inheritance can be checked against a known pedigree, or when comparing SV caller concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-joint-genotyping))
- **long-read-sv-validation**: Use when SV calls (e.g. deletions from Supernova2 assemblies or SvABA/Long Ranger callsets) must be independently validated against long-read sequencing data before benchmarking or release. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/long-read-sv-validation))
- **long-read-tandem-repeat-profiling**: Use when haplotype-resolved BAM files from ONT or PacBio whole-genome sequencing must be scanned de novo for tandem repeats with motif length &gt;6 bp (mini-satellites) without prior motif or locus knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-tandem-repeat-profiling))
- **long-read-taxonomic-alignment**: Use when quality-controlled ARG-containing long reads (Nanopore or PacBio) need candidate species assignments and alignment scores against a GTDB-derived reference, as the host-collection step of an ARG profiling pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-taxonomic-alignment))
- **long-read-tcr-sequence-detection**: Use when you have ONT (or PacBio) FASTQs from targeted amplification of a 10x Genomics V(DJ) cDNA library and need per-cell-barcode TCR calls, e.g. tumor-infiltrating T cell TCR profiling with nanoranger. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-tcr-sequence-detection))
- **long-read-telomere-sequencing-table-parsing**: Use when you have demultiplexed telomap TSV output from WGS or telobait-captured long-read data and need per-patient mean telomere length metrics for group-level statistics such as age correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-telomere-sequencing-table-parsing))
- **long-read-to-contig-alignment**: Use when corrected long reads must be mapped to haplotype-collapsed contigs to obtain pileup-based heterozygous SNP sites for read grouping and inconsistent-overlap detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-to-contig-alignment))
- **long-read-to-contig-mapping-statistics**: Use when a labelled ntLink PAF mapping file (read-to-contig mappings generated with paf=True) must be summarized to validate long-read mappings against expected statistics from the ntLink article's Table 5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-to-contig-mapping-statistics))
- **long-read-to-reference-alignment**: Use when full-length (~1500 bp) 16S PacBio CCS reads must be mapped to a single 16S reference to quantify per-position substitutions that distinguish intragenomic 16S gene copies within one genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/long-read-to-reference-alignment))
- **long-read-transcript-alignment**: Use when input is PacBio Iso-seq full-length transcript FASTA (CCS reads) that must be aligned to a reference genome (e.g., hg38.fa) with splice-aware annotation of transcripts, junctions, and read classifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-alignment))
- **long-read-transcript-alignment-filtering**: Use when raw PacBio/ONT FASTQ reads with single-cell barcodes must be splice-aligned to a reference genome and filtered to high-confidence full-length spliced isoforms before per-cell-type isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-transcript-alignment-filtering))
- **long-read-transcript-catalog-analysis**: Use when you have a filtered long-read isoform database (e.g., IsoPops DB with per-isoform read counts) and need to quantify how each gene's expression is distributed across its isoforms or rank genes by isoform diversity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-catalog-analysis))
- **long-read-transcript-error-correction**: Use when long noisy reads from targeted PacBio Iso-Seq (or ONT/R2C2 CCS-style) sequencing of highly similar gene copies or amplicons must be error-corrected and collapsed into finished consensus transcripts, including isoform-capture versus sequencing-depth analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-error-correction))
- **long-read-transcript-isoform-quantification**: Use when you need isoform-level expression estimates from ONT direct-RNA (RNA004) reads aligned to a transcriptome, or when validating a nanopore simulator by comparing ESPRESSO-estimated isoform abundances to ground-truth transcript abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcript-isoform-quantification))
- **long-read-transcriptome-annotation**: Use when you have a merged StringTie GTF assembly from hybrid long-read (PacBio) plus Illumina RNA-seq and need coding/noncoding classification, GTF reconciliation against a reference annotation, and identification of novel genes absent from that reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-annotation))
- **long-read-transcriptome-isoform-detection**: Use when analyzing long-read RNA-seq (nanopore or PacBio Iso-Seq) BAM/GFF3/BED6 alignments of selected genes or spike-ins (e.g., SIRVs) to identify and quantify known or novel isoforms and tune detection thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-isoform-detection))
- **long-read-transcriptome-reconstruction**: Use when you have BAM files of aligned full-length LRTS transcripts (e.g. PacBio SequelII hepatocyte CTL/VPA data) and a gff3/gtf reference annotation, and need gene/transcript reconstruction with novel-transcript classification and tunable filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-reconstruction))
- **long-read-transcriptome-sequencing-qc**: Use when raw LRTS bam files (aligned full-length transcripts, e.g. PacBio SequelII IsoSeq data) need QC assessment, filter tagging, or when a saturation/depth analysis is needed to decide if rare transcripts (e.g. 1 TPM) are reliably observed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transcriptome-sequencing-qc))
- **long-read-transposable-element-calling**: Use when you have a long-read BAM/CRAM aligned to a reference genome and need to detect non-reference TE insertions, or when benchmarking sTELLeR against TLDR/xTEA on PacBio data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-transposable-element-calling))
- **long-read-variant-phasing-haplotagging**: Use when calling SNVs from ONT long-read BAM alignments where low-quality candidate loci must be resolved via haplotype-aware consensus sequences, or when phased, haplotagged alignments are needed to build per-haplotype local assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-variant-phasing-haplotagging))
- **long-read-vs-short-read-isoform-benchmarking**: Use when a long-read study reports isoforms for a complex, multi-isoform gene locus and you need to test whether per-cell short-read Illumina assembly recovers those same isoforms or produces contigs absent from the long-read set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-vs-short-read-isoform-benchmarking))
- **long-read-vs-short-read-splicing-comparison**: Use when you have a reconstructed long-read transcriptome (e.g. IsoTools pickle from PacBio LRTS) plus paired short-read RNA-seq, and want to know whether PSI values agree per splicing event category. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-vs-short-read-splicing-comparison))
- **long-read-wgs-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw long-read whole-genome reads (PacBio HiFi or targeted ONT FASTQ/BAM) from human rare-disease samples must be turned into aligned BAMs, phased variant calls, de novo assemblies and methylation profiles via a containerized Nextflow run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/long-read-wgs-pipeline-execution))
